- **homework-coach**: Use when a parent or child wants homework help — math, reading, writing, science, study skills. Never gives the final answers to assigned problems or writes graded work. - **homosapiens**: This skill should be used before publishing any text other people will read (PR titles and bodies, Jira comments, commit messages, docs, Confluence pages) and whenever the user asks to remove AI tells, humanize a text, or make writing sound less AI-generated. It rewrites text to strip AI writing patterns and add human voice without changing what it says. Embedded inside another tool (such as tribunal's PR reviews) it returns only the final text. ([xmartlabs/agent-skills](https://github.com/xmartlabs/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/homosapiens/skills/homosapiens) · ★2)
- **homowabian**: Register control for WabbleSpec output tone. Switches between lite/full/ultra/normal response modes. One CLAUDE.md line activates. Module exists for documentation only — complexity has not grown enough to warrant skill logic. ([GG-Santos/wabblespec](https://github.com/GG-Santos/wabblespec/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/homowabian) · 2 copies)
- **homozygous-allele-verification-across-catalog-span**: Use when calling progeny indel genotypes from VCF evidence at known parental InDel catalog sites and needing to distinguish homozygous from heterozygous calls without direct VCF support for the homozygous state. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/homozygous-allele-verification-across-catalog-span))
- **homozygous-discovery-rate-analysis**: Use when benchmarking SNV-caller sensitivity on ONT Dorado sup-basecalled data, specifically when computing homozygous discovery rate (HDR) against GIAB truth sets for HG002/HG003/HG004 and comparing miniSNV with Clair3 and Longshot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/homozygous-discovery-rate-analysis))
- **hon-maguro**: Evaluate any project against fixed "Hon Maguro" quality bars via bin/maguro (init/links/eval), optional Lighthouse median for SCORE1, and a capped fix loop (max 2 rounds). Binary pass/fail — no partial credit. Use when the user says "hon maguro", "eval this project", "quality bar check", "is this project ready", "audit code quality", or wants a pass/fail verdict against Hon Maguro standards. Do not use for generic Lighthouse/PageSpeed-only or open-ended "inspect and fix" requests unless they ask for Hon Maguro / these bars. ([stevederico/hon-maguro](https://github.com/stevederico/hon-maguro/tree/HEAD/skills/hon-maguro) · ★1)

HEARTBEAT.md is excluded. Proves each check fails on a real past mistake. ([tmcfarlane/team-avatar](https://github.com/tmcfarlane/team-avatar/tree/HEAD/plugins/team-avatar/skills/honor-code) · ★114)

- **honorarios-da-fazenda**: Honorários nas causas em que a Fazenda é parte, pelo CPC 85 verbatim: as cinco faixas do §3º (10-20% até 200 SM · 8-10% até 2.000 · 5-8% até 20.000 · 3-5% até 100.000 · 1-3% acima), a aplicação escalonada do §5º — faixa inicial e, no que exceder, a subsequente, como imposto de renda, nunca percentual único sobre o todo —, o §4º (líquida aplica desde logo, ilíquida só na liquidação), o §6º (vale inclusive na improcedência) e o §6º-A (é PROIBIDA a apreciação equitativa quando o valor é líquido ou liquidável, salvo o §8º).
